{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EmV9qbft8YbcGxGhhaUD8N","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t@~/.claude/gsd-core/references/response-language-directive.md\n2\t\n3\t<purpose>\n4\tOrchestrate parallel codebase mapper agents to analyze codebase and produce structured documents in .planning/codebase/\n5\t\n6\tEach agent has fresh context, explores a specific focus area, and **writes documents directly**. The orchestrator only receives confirmation + line counts, then writes a summary.\n7\t\n8\tOutput: .planning/codebase/ folder with 7 structured documents about the codebase state.\n9\t</purpose>\n10\t\n11\t<available_agent_types>\n12\tValid GSD subagent types (use exact names — do not fall back to 'general-purpose'):\n13\t- gsd-codebase-mapper — Maps project structure and dependencies\n14\t</available_agent_types>\n15\t\n16\t<philosophy>\n17\t**Why dedicated mapper agents:**\n18\t- Fresh context per domain (no token contamination)\n19\t- Agents write documents directly (no context transfer back to orchestrator)\n20\t- Orchestrator only summarizes what was created (minimal context usage)\n21\t- Faster execution (agents run simultaneously)\n22\t\n23\t**Document quality over length:**\n24\tInclude enough detail to be useful as reference. Prioritize practical examples (especially code patterns) over arbitrary brevity.\n25\t\n26\t**Always include file paths:**\n27\tDocuments are reference material for Claude when planning/executing. Always include actual file paths formatted with backticks: `src/services/user.ts`.\n28\t</philosophy>\n29\t\n30\t<process>\n31\t\n32\t<step name=\"parse_paths_flag\" priority=\"first\">\n33\tParse an optional `--paths <p1,p2,...>` argument. When supplied (by the\n34\tpost-execute codebase-drift gate in `/gsd-execute-phase` or by a user running\n35\t`/gsd-map-codebase --paths apps/accounting,packages/ui`), the workflow\n36\toperates in **incremental-remap mode**:\n37\t\n38\t- Pass `--paths <p1>,<p2>,...` through to each spawned `gsd-codebase-mapper`\n39\t  agent's prompt. Agents scope their Glob/Grep/Bash exploration to the listed\n40\t  repo-relative prefixes only — no whole-repo scan.\n41\t- Reject path values that contain `..`, start with `/`, or include shell\n42\t  metacharacters (`;`, `` ` ``, `$`, `&`, `|`, `<`, `>`). If all provided\n43\t  paths are invalid, fall back to a normal whole-repo run.\n44\t- The `last_mapped_commit` baseline is NOT the mapper's job. It is stamped\n45\t  deterministically by the `stamp_codebase_map` step below, on every run,\n46\t  incremental or full. See that step for why.\n47\t\n48\t**Explicit contract — propagate `--paths` through a single normalized\n49\tvariable.** Downstream steps (`spawn_agents`, `sequential_mapping`, and any\n50\tAgent-mode prompt construction) MUST use `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` to ensure every\n51\tmapper receives the same deterministic scope. Without this contract\n52\tincremental-remap can silently regress to a whole-repo scan.\n53\t\n54\t```bash\n55\t# Validated, comma-separated paths (empty if --paths absent or all rejected):\n56\tSCOPED_PATHS=\"<validated paths or empty>\"\n57\tif [ -n \"$SCOPED_PATHS\" ]; It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. Review the flagged content above\n2. If these are real secrets, they must be removed before committing\n3. Consider adding sensitive files to Claude Code \"Deny\" permissions\n\nPausing before commit. Reply \"safe to proceed\" if the flagged content is not actually sensitive, or edit the files first.\n```\n\nWait for user confirmation before continuing to commit_codebase_map.\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=false:**\n\nContinue to commit_codebase_map.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"commit_codebase_map\">\nCommit the codebase map:\n\n```bash\ngsd_run query commit \"docs: map existing codebase\" --files .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\nContinue to offer_next.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"offer_next\">\nPresent completion summary and next steps.\n\n**Get line counts:**\n```bash\nwc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\n**Output format:**\n\n```\nCodebase mapping complete.\n\nCreated .planning/codebase/:\n- STACK.md ([N] lines) - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01W4xSTQ4NFBL1g1r9CA23EV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\texport type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'\n2\t\n3\texport const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'theme'\n4\t\n5\t/** The current theme, read from html[data-theme]. Only 'dark' counts as dark. */\n6\texport function readTheme(): Theme {\n7\t  return document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n8\t}\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * True when the visitor chose a theme but storage refused it. Storage is the record of an\n12\t * explicit choice, so without this flag a blocked-storage visitor would look like one who\n13\t * never chose, and the next OS scheme change would silently undo their choice.\n14\t */\n15\tlet unsavedChoice = false\n16\t\n17\t/**\n18\t * Switch the theme: the attribute is written first so the switch always works, then the\n19\t * choice is stored. When storage is blocked the choice lasts for the page session only, and\n20\t * the OS scheme no longer overrides it.\n21\t */\n22\texport function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {\n23\t  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n24\t  try {\n25\t    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme)\n26\t    unsavedChoice = false\n27\t  } catch {\n28\t    // Storage blocked (private mode, policy): the in-memory choice still applies.\n29\t    unsavedChoice = true\n30\t  }\n31\t}\n32\t\n33\tconst DARK_QUERY = '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'\n34\t\n35\tfunction isTheme(value: unknown): value is Theme {\n36\t  return value === 'light' || value === 'dark'\n37\t}\n38\t\n39\tfunction storedTheme(): Theme | null {\n40\t  try {\n41\t    const value = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)\n42\t    return isTheme(value) ? value : null\n43\t  } catch {\n44\t    return null\n45\t  }\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\tfunction systemTheme(): Theme {\n49\t  try {\n50\t    return window.matchMedia(DARK_QUERY).matches ? 'dark' : 'light'\n51\t  } catch {\n52\t    return 'light'\n53\t  }\n54\t}\n55\t\n56\t/** Write the attribute without storing anything, and only when it would change. */\n57\tfunction syncAttribute(theme: Theme): void {\n58\t  if (readTheme() !== theme || !document.documentElement.hasAttribute('data-theme')) {\n59\t    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n60\t  }\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\t/**\n64\t * Subscribe to theme changes made outside the toggle: another tab changing or clearing the\n65\t * stored choice, and the OS scheme changing while nothing is stored. Both are folded into\n66\t * html[data-theme], which stays the single source of truth, and `onChange` fires when that\n67\t * attribute changes for any reason. Returns the cleanup.\n68\t */\n69\texport function subscribeTheme(onChange: () => void): () => void {\n70\t  const observer = new MutationObserver(onChange)\n71\t  observer.observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-theme'] })\n72\t\n73\t  // `storage` fires in other tabs only. A null key means the whole store was cleared.\n74\t  const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {\n75\t    if (event.key !== null && event.key !== THEME_STORAGE_KEY) return\n76\t    // Storage events only fire when storage works, and it is now the record of the choice.\n77\t    unsavedChoice = false\n78\t    syncAttribute(storedTheme() ?? systemTheme())\n79\t  }\n80\t  window.addEventListener('storage', onStorage)\n81\t\n82\t  let mql: MediaQueryList | null = null\n83\t  try {\n84\t    mql = window.matchMedia(DARK_QUERY)\n85\t  } catch {\n86\t    // matchMedia missing or blocked: OS scheme changes are simply not followed.\n87\t  }\n88\t  const onSystemChange = () => {\n89\t    if (!unsavedChoice && storedTheme() === null) syncAttribute(systemTheme())\n90\t  }\n91\t  mql?.addEventListener('change', onSystemChange)\n92\t\n93\t  return () => {\n94\t    observer.disconnect()\n95\t    window.removeEventListener('storage', onStorage)\n96\t    mql?.removeEventListener('change', onSystemChange)\n97\t  }\n98\t}\n99\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01GU9MRpTmP7m7x9PVZ8oMfs","agent_id":"af135525dd89e2bf6","session_id":"c554a6fb-e79e-470f-8309-b1d2a493e333","uuid":"378bf2c7-6447-41c2-922b-e7f9d1365a1b","timestamp":"2026-11-20T05:14:54.446Z","tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01W4xSTQ4NFBL1g1r9CA23EV","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}],"subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Refresh tech+arch codebase map"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016qLE3hBVdLeaYiZVZg66Jq","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer,          |\n31\t|             SkipLink, Wordmark                                        |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons)                  |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, useTheme,  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t|  cn, a11y)                    |                                      |\n42\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n43\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n44\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n45\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t## Import rules\n49\t\n50\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n51\t\n52\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n53\t\n54\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n55\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n56\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n57\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n58\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n59\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n60\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n61\t\n62\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n63\t\n64\t## Folder structure\n65\t\n66\t```text\n67\trepo root\n68\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set and\n69\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free export); the og\n70\t|                            image and robots.txt are launch assets, not yet\n71\t|                            shipped\n72\t|-- index.html               meta description, inline theme-init script and the\n73\t|                            static hero shell; social tags and noscript\n74\t|                            content for the other sections arrive with the\n75\t|                            launch assets\n76\t|-- 404.html                 second HTML entry: the not-found page that\n77\t|                            GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths\n78\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n79\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n80\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n81\t`-- src/\n82\t    |-- main.tsx\n83\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n84\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n85\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n86\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav,\n87\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink, Wordmark\n88\t    |-- components/\n89\t    |   |-- ui/              CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons/ (inline SVG\n90\t    |   |                    brand marks and UI glyphs)\n91\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored and logged in an ADR (TextEffect, kept\n92\t    |                        unrendered for the hero role-line effect)\n93\t    |-- anim/\n94\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n95\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n96\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n97\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n98\t    |   |-- MotionFeatures.tsx   LazyMotion provider with the async feature chunk\n99\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence,\n100\t    |   |                    NavIndicator, PillMorph)\n101\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb and other\n102\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n103\t    |-- content/\n104\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n105\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n106\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n107\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n108\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n109\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useTheme\n110\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n111\t    |-- styles/\n112\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n113\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n114\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n115\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n116\t```\n117\t\n118\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n119\t\n120\t## Data flow\n121\t\n122\tBuild time:\n123\t\n124\t```text\n125\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n126\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n127\t```\n128\t\n129\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n130\t\n131\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n132\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n133\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n134\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n135\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n136\t\n137\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n138\t\n139\t## Content model\n140\t\n141\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n142\t\n143\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n144\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n145\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n146\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n147\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n148\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n149\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n150\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n151\t\n152\tRules:\n153\t\n154\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n155\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n156\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n157\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n158\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n159\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n160\t\n161\t## Design tokens\n162\t\n163\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n164\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n165\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n166\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n167\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n168\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n169\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n170\t\n171\t## Animation layer\n172\t\n173\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n174\t\n175\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n176\t|-------------------|-------|\n177\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n178\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n179\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n180\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n181\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n182\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n183\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n184\t\n185\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n186\t\n187\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n188\t\n189\t## Reduced motion\n190\t\n191\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n192\t\n193\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n194\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n195\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n196\t\n197\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs, and no wrapper applies a hidden initial state in CSS. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query, so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload in both directions. Turning reduced motion off mid-visit also loads the motion feature chunk, which reduced-motion visitors otherwise never download.\n198\t\n199\t## Assets\n200\t\n201\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n202\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n203\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) and UI glyphs (close, chevrons, download) are inline SVG components under src/components/ui/icons/. No icon package is installed. See ADR-0005 and ADR-0010 in the ADR log.\n204\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is the owner's phone-number-free export of the resume. The phone-number gate reads its text layer, link annotations and metadata, and rejects a stand-in file.\n205\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n206\t\n207\t## Build and deploy\n208\t\n209\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. The build has a second HTML entry, 404.html, a static not-found page that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n210\t\n211\t## Quality gates\n212\t\n213\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n214\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n215\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n216\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n217\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n218\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n219\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n220\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n221\t| Token contrast test | `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in any theme |\n222\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n223\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n224\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link, or a PDF's text layer, link annotations or metadata does (read with pdfjs-dist), or the PDF cannot be read, or resume.pdf is not a real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of text) |\n225\t| Image check | `scripts/check-images.mjs` over dist, public and src | A shipped raster is not AVIF or WebP, carries Exif or XMP, or is unreferenced, or a source image is not imported |\n226\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n227\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n228\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n229\t\n230\t`npm run verify` chains the first eleven rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n231\t\n232\t## Stack\n233\t\n234\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at scaffold.\n235\t\n236\t| Technology | Version | Purpose |\n237\t|------------|---------|---------|\n238\t| Vite | ^8.3.4 | Build and dev server |\n239\t| React and React DOM | ^19.3.0 | UI |\n240\t| The site makes no `fetch`, XHR or WebSocket calls. Searches for `fetch(` and `XMLHttpRequest` in `src/` return nothing. `docs/architecture.md` states the runtime uses no network beyond same-origin assets.\n- Outbound links only (anchor navigation, not API calls):\n  - GitHub profile, LinkedIn profile, Instagram profile: `href` values defined in `src/content/contacts.ts` (`kind` values `github`, `linkedin`, `instagram`), rendered by `src/layout/Footer.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx` and the hero/contact sections.\n  - Email: a `mailto:` link from the `email` entry in `src/content/contacts.ts`. The same address appears as a literal `mailto:` href in `index.html` (static hero shell). Keep both in sync when the address changes.\n\n**Build-time (CI and developer tooling):**\n- npm registry (`registry.npmjs.org`) - Dependency install via `npm ci` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`). Resolved URLs are pinned in `package-lock.json`.\n- Playwright browser CDN - `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium` in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (e2e job) and the same step in `deploy.yml` (e2e job). Downloads Chromium at CI time only.\n- Motion primitives registry - `https://motion-primitives.com/c/<name>.json` is referenced in `.claude/CLAUDE.md` as the source for the vendored component. Not called by the app or CI. Vendored source lives in `src/components/motion-primitives/`.\n\n**Image and font delivery:**\n- No external CDN. Fonts come from `@fontsource-variable/archivo` in the npm package and are bundled (`src/main.tsx`). Images are imported as modules and converted at build time by `vite-imagetools` (`src/content/images.ts`). Nothing is fetched from a third-party image host.\n\n## Data Storage\n\n**Databases:**\n- Not applicable. No database, ORM or query client is installed.\n\n**File Storage:**\n- Local filesystem only, in the build. Static assets in `src/assets/images/` and `public/` (including `public/resume.pdf` and `public/favicon.svg`). Deployed as static files.\n\n**Caching:**\n- None at the app level. The browser caches hashed Vite assets. The GitHub Pages CDN serves the output.\n\n**Browser storage (client-side, not a backend):**\n- `localStorage` key `theme` (value `light` or `dark`). Read in the inline script in `index.html` and `404.html` before first paint, and read and written in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`THEME_STORAGE_KEY`). Storage is wrapped in try/catch, so blocked storage falls back to an in-memory choice.\n- Clipboard: `navigator.clipboard.writeText` with an `execCommand('copy')` fallback in `src/lib/copy.ts`. Only the public email address is written. The app never reads the clipboard.\n- `window.location.href` set to the `mailto:` link when copy fails (`src/lib/copy.ts` `openMail`).\n\n## Authentication & Identity\n\n**Auth Provider:**\n- Not applicable. The site has no accounts, sessions, login or access control.\n\n**Deployment identity (CI only):**\n- GitHub OIDC token for Pages deployment. `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` grants `id-token: write` and `pages: write` only to the `deploy` job, under the `github-pages` environment. Build and e2e jobs get `contents: read` (and `pages: read` for the build job).\n- No secrets are referenced in the workflows. No `secrets.*` or `GITHUB_TOKEN` values are used by the application.\n\n## Monitoring & Observability\n\n**Error Tracking:**\n- None. No Sentry, analytics or RUM SDK in `package.json`, `index.html` or `src/`.\n\n**Logs:**\n- None at runtime. The app emits no `console` logging by design; CI output is the only log surface (`npm run verify` and Playwright list reporter, `playwright.config.ts`).\n\n**Analytics:**\n- None detected.\n\n## CI/CD & Deployment\n\n**Hosting:**\n- GitHub Pages. Project-path base in CI (`VITE_BASE: /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/` in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`). Documented cutover to the user-site root in `docs/deploy-runbook.md` and `docs/adr/0003-github-pages-source-and-cutover.md`.\n- Post-deploy check in `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`: `curl` the deployed `page_url` and `grep` for the owner's name, with retries.\n\n**CI Pipeline:**\n- GitHub Actions. Two workflows:\n  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` - On every `push` and `pull_request`. Job `verify`: checkout, setup-node (`lts/*`, npm cache), `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, Playwright Chromium install, `npm run test:e2e`.\n  - `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - On `push` to `main` and `workflow_dispatch`. Jobs: `build` (verify plus `configure-pages` and `upload-pages-artifact` of `./dist`), `e2e` (needs build), `deploy` (needs build and e2e; `deploy-pages`, then the curl check).\n- Actions are pinned by commit SHA with version comments.\n- Concurrency: `ci-${{ github.ref }}` cancels in-progress runs (ci.yml); the `pages` group does not cancel in-progress deploys (deploy.yml).\n\n## Environment Configuration\n\n**Required env vars:**\n- None for the app at runtime.\n- `VITE_BASE` - Optional. Build base path. Default `/` locally; CI sets the project path. Read in `vite.config.ts` and `playwright.config.ts`.\n- `E2E_BASE_URL` - Optional. Run Playwright against a live URL (`playwright.config.ts`).\n- `CI` - Set by GitHub Actions. Changes Playwright `forbidOnly` and server reuse.\n\n**Secrets location:**\n- No application secrets. No `.env` file is tracked in the repo, and `.gitignore` excludes `*.local`. No secret values are read or referenced by source code. If a deploy secret is ever needed, the workflows in `.github/workflows/` are the place to add it through the GitHub repository secret store.\n\n## Webhooks & Callbacks\n\n**Incoming:**\n- None. refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Build time (Node, Vite 8)                                           │\n│  vite.config.ts  ->  index.html + 404.html  ->  dist/                │\n│  src/content/*.ts (typed data) + src/assets/images (imagetools)      │\n└───────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                │ static files (GitHub Pages)\n┌───────────────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Browser (single-page React app, no router, no network calls)        │\n│                                                                      │\n│  src/main.tsx  ->  src/app/App.tsx  ->  Providers  ->  Page.tsx      │\n│                                                                      │\n│  ┌────────────┐ ┌─────────────────────────┐ ┌────────────────────┐   │\n│  │ layout/    │ │ sections/ (7 sections)  │ │ components/ui/     │   │\n│  │ SiteHeader │ │ Hero, Experience,       │ │ ThemeToggle, Toast │   │\n│  │ PillNav    │ │ Projects, About, Skills │ │ CtaLink, icons/    │   │\n│  │ Footer     │ │ Education, Contact      │ │                    │   │\n│  └─────┬──────┘ └───────────┬─────────────┘ └──────────┬─────────┘   │\n│        └────────────────────┴───────────────────────────┘             │\n│                             │ props only                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────────────┐  │\n│  │ anim/ (barrel `@/anim`): motion/react + @react-spring/web       │  │\n│  │ components/motion-primitives/ (vendored TextEffect)             │  │\n│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │\n│  │ hooks/ (useTheme, useActiveSection)   lib/ (theme, copy, utils) │  │\n│  │ styles/ (tokens.css -> Tailwind @theme inline, base, interact)  │  │\n│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │\n│        Browser APIs only: localStorage, matchMedia, IntersectionObserver,│\n│        navigator.clipboard, <dialog>.showModal()                     │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static single-page React app with a layered, props-down component tree. Content is typed TypeScript data, and all presentation is driven by CSS-variable design tokens.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- No router, no global store, no data fetching. The only navigation is in-page anchors to section ids (`src/layout/nav-items.ts`).\n- All copy is typed data in `src/content/`, checked against `src/content/schema.ts` with `satisfies`. Only `src/app/Page.tsx` imports that data.\n- Theme is stored in `html[data-theme]`, which is the single source of truth. An inline script sets it before first paint.\n- Animation is isolated behind one barrel (`src/anim/index.ts`). Lint rules in `.oxlintrc.json` enforce the import boundaries.\n- Build produces two HTML entries (`index.html`, `404.html`) for GitHub Pages.\n\n## Layers\n\n**app (composition root):**\n- Purpose: Mount React, wrap providers, assemble the page.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`, `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Contains: Root render, provider stack, the one page component.\n- Depends on: `sections/`, `layout/`, `anim/` (barrel), `content/` (only `Page.tsx`), `hooks/useActiveSection`.\n- Used by: Nothing (top of the tree).\n\n**sections (feature UI):**\n- Purpose: Render one page section each from props.\n- Location: `src/sections/Hero/`, `src/sections/Experience/`, `src/sections/Projects/` (Projects, Sleeve, ProjectDialog, project-types), `src/sections/About/`, `src/sections/Skills/`, `src/sections/Education/`, `src/sections/Contact/`\n- Contains: Components, section-local types and dialog state.\n- Depends on: `layout/`, `components/ui/`, `anim/` barrel, `lib/`, types from `src/content/schema.ts` (type-only).\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n\n**layout (chrome):**\n- Purpose: Header, navigation, footer, layout primitives.\n- Location: `src/layout/` (`SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`)\n- Contains: Navigation, skip link, container and section wrappers.\n- Depends on: `anim/` barrel, `components/ui/`, `hooks/`, `lib/`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `sections/`.\n\n**components (reusable UI):**\n- Purpose: Shared UI primitives and vendored motion components.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/` (`ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `CtaLink.tsx`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `useToast.ts`, `icons/`), `src/components/motion-primitives/` (vendored `text-effect.tsx`)\n- Contains: Toggle, toast system, link CTA, inline SVG icons, copy hook.\n- Depends on: `anim/` barrel, `hooks/`, `lib/`.\n- Used by: `layout/`, `sections/`.\n\n**anim (animation layer, the only motion import site):**\n- Purpose: Wrap `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web` behind semantic components.\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n  - `index.ts` - public barrel (the only allowed import path).\n  - `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts` - reduced-motion source and config for both libraries.\n  - `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts` - lazy `LazyMotion` feature chunk.\n  - `tokens.ts` - spring and duration tokens in both libraries' formats.\n  - `declarative/` - `ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx` (motion-owned).\n  - `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` (react-spring-owned).\n- Contains: Providers, wrappers, tokens.\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `components/motion-primitives/`. Must not import `sections/`, `layout/` or `content/` (`.oxlintrc.json` override for `**/anim/**`).\n- Used by: `app/`, `layout/`, `components/`, `sections/`.\n\n**hooks and lib (shared logic):**\n- Purpose: Stateful hooks and framework-free helpers.\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useTheme.ts`, `useActiveSection.ts`), `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`)\n- Contains: Theme read/write and cross-tab sync (`lib/theme.ts`), clipboard and mailto (`lib/copy.ts`), `cn()` (`lib/utils.ts`), scroll-spy (`hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n- Depends on: Browser APIs only.\n- Used by: `components/`, `layout/`, `sections/`, `app/`.\n\n**styles and assets:**\n- Purpose: Design system and images.\n- Location: `src/styles/` (`index.css`, `tokens.css`, `base.css`, `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`), `src/assets/images/`\n- Contains: Five role colors per theme (text, background, primary, secondary, accent) and derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`. Tailwind v4 `@theme inline` mapping. Reduced-motion guard in `base.css`.\n- Used by: All components.\n\n**content (typed data):**\n- Purpose: All site copy and structured data.\n- Location: `src/content/` (`schema.ts`, `index.ts`, `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`)\n- Contains: Plain TS objects validated by `satisfies` and typed in `schema.ts`.\n- Depends on: `src/assets/images/` (via `images.ts` imports).\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only.\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (initial page load)\n\n1. The browser requests `index.html` (or `404.html` for unknown paths on GitHub Pages). An inline script in `index.html` reads `localStorage['theme']` and falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, then sets `html[data-theme]` before first paint.\n2. `index.html` loads `src/main.tsx` as a module. It imports the Archivo font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`.\n3. `src/main.tsx` calls `createRoot(#root).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)`.\n4. `src/app/App.tsx` renders `Providers` (`src/app/Providers.tsx`), which nests `MotionPreferenceProvider`, then `MotionFeatures` (`LazyMotion`), then `ToastProvider`.\n5. `src/app/Page.tsx` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives the email and other links, calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)`, and renders `SiteHeader`, `<main>` with seven sections, `Footer`, and `PillNav`.\n6. Sections render from props. `Hero` gets `profile` and `email`, `Experience` gets `entries`, `Projects` gets `projects`, and so on.\n\n### Theme toggle\n\n1. `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` uses `useTheme()` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `toggle()` calls `applyTheme()` in `src/lib/theme.ts`, which sets `data-theme` first, then writes `localStorage['theme']`.\n3. `subscribeTheme()` watches the `data-theme` attribute with a `MutationObserver`, the `storage` event (other tabs) and the `prefers-color-scheme` media query, and keeps the attribute in sync.\n4. `ThemeThumb` (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`) animates the toggle thumb with react-spring.\n\n### Scroll spy and navigation\n\n1. `useActiveSection` (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`) runs one `IntersectionObserver` over the section ids. It is a no-op when the API is missing.\n2. `Page.tsx` passes `activeId` and `lockTo` to `SiteHeader` and `PillNav`. Clicking a nav item calls `lockTo` so the highlight does not flicker during smooth scroll.\n\n### Copy email\n\n1. `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` calls `copyText()` in `src/lib/copy.ts`. This uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, then falls back to `execCommand('copy')`.\n2. On failure, `openMail()` sets `window.location.href` to the `mailto:` link.\n3. The result is announced through `ToastProvider` (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts` context).\n\n### Project detail\n\n1. `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` holds the selected index in local state. `Sleeve.tsx` renders each card and triggers selection.\n2. `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` calls `dialog.showModal()` in an effect, and restores focus to the previously active element on close.\n\n### Motion preference\n\n1. `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` reads `prefers-reduced-motion` and the optional site override.\n2. `MotionPreferenceProvider` configures motion's `MotionConfig` and calls react-spring's `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`.\n3. `MotionFeatures` delays loading the `motion` feature chunk while reduced motion is on, and releases it when reduced motion is turned off.\n\n**State Management:**\n- Theme: `html[data-theme]` attribute plus `localStorage`. Module-level flag `unsavedChoice` in `src/lib/theme.ts` tracks a choice that could not be stored.\n- Motion: `MotionPreferenceProvider` React context (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`).\n- Toasts: `ToastContext` (`src/components/ui/useToast.ts`), provided by `ToastProvider`.\n- Active section: local hook state in `useActiveSection`.\n- Project selection and dialog: local state in `src/sections/Projects/`.\n- There is no global store. Props flow down.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content schema:**\n- Purpose: One type per content shape, so missing fields fail typecheck.\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, `DateRange`).\n- Pattern: `satisfies` on each data module, assembled in `src/content/index.ts` as `siteContent`.\n\n**ImageAsset:**\n- Purpose: One image contract: `src`, `alt`, `width`, `height`, optional AVIF and WebP `sources`.\n- Examples: `src/content/images.ts` (`aboutPhoto`, employer logos built with `logo()`).\n- Pattern: imagetools `?as=picture` imports, converted to `ImageAsset`. Empty `alt` marks decorative images.\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: Semantic animation API that hides library primitives.\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts` exports `ThemeThumb`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `MotionFeatures`, `useMotionPreference`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`.\n- Pattern: One library per element or property. Each wrapper reads the motion preference and renders an instant version when reduced.\n\n**Theme API:**\n- Purpose: Read, set and subscribe to the theme without React context.\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts` (`readTheme`, `applyTheme`, `subscribeTheme`), `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`.\n- Pattern: The DOM attribute is the source of truth. Storage is the record of an explicit choice.\n\n**Vendored motion primitive:**\n- Purpose: Hero role-line text effect.\n- Examples: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`.\n- Pattern: Vendored source treated as owned code. Import-boundary lint rules are turned off for this folder (`.oxlintrc.json` override).\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser document (dev and production):**\n- Location: `index.html` (main entry, static hero shell plus inline theme script), `404.html` (not-found entry).\n- Triggers: Any page load on GitHub Pages (`404.html` for unknown paths).\n- Responsibilities: Pre-paint theme, static shell, module script load.\n\n**JS bootstrap:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`.\n- Triggers: `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">` in `index.html`.\n- Responsibilities: Font and global CSS import, React root creation.\n\n**Build and dev:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts` (`vite`, `vite build`, `vite preview`).\n- Triggers: `npm run dev`, `npm run build`, `npm run preview`.\n- Responsibilities: Base path from `VITE_BASE`, React and Tailwind transform, imagetools, `@` alias, two HTML inputs.\n\n**CI and deploy:**\n- Location: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (every push and PR), `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` (push to `main`).\n- Triggers: GitHub events.\n- Responsibilities: `npm run verify`, Playwright e2e, Pages artifact and deploy.\n\n**Test entries:**\n- Location: `vitest.config.ts` (unit, `tests/unit/`), `playwright.config.ts` (e2e, `tests/e2e/`).\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers or service worker in `src/`. Build runs in Node.\n- **Global state:** Module-level `unsavedChoice` in `src/lib/theme.ts`. Module-level feature-loaded flag in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`. The `html[data-theme]` attribute is global DOM state.\n- **Circular imports:** None observed. Boundary rules in `.oxlintrc.json` forbid `anim/` importing `sections/`, `layout/` or `content/`, and forbid anything but `Page.tsx` importing `content/`.\n- **Import boundaries:** Only `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`. Enforced by oxlint `no-restricted-imports` (`npm run lint`).\n- **Static only:** No server, no API, no SSR. Anything added that needs a backend would be new architecture.\n- **Two HTML entries:** `vite.config.ts` `build.rolldownOptions.input` lists `index.html` and `404.html`. Both must keep the same theme-init script (`tests/unit/theme-script.test.ts` checks parity).\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Animation library imported outside the barrel\n\n**What happens:** Code imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` from a section or layout file, or imports `@/anim/physics/...` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the one-library-per-element rule and the lazy-feature design, and fails `npm run lint`.\n**Do this instead:** Import from `@/anim` (`src/anim/index.ts`). Add a semantic wrapper in `src/anim/` if the barrel lacks one.\n\n### Content imported from components\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout component imports `@/content` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Violates the rule that only `src/app/Page.tsx` reads content data. It couples presentation to specific copy and breaks the props-down contract.\n**Do this instead:** Pass data as props from `src/app/Page.tsx` (`src/app/Page.tsx` pattern). Use types from `src/content/schema.ts` with `import type`.\n\n### Hard-coded colors or Tailwind palette utilities\n\n**What happens:** Raw hex, rgb or hsl values, or default Tailwind palette classes (for example `bg-blue-500`) in components.\n**Why it's wrong:** `scripts/check-colors.mjs` fails the build, and it breaks the five-role token system.\n**Do this instead:** Use the semantic utilities mapped in `src/styles/index.css` (`@theme inline`), and change values only in `src/styles/tokens.css`.\n\n### Writing theme state directly to the DOM or storage\n\n**What happens:** Code sets `data-theme` or `localStorage['theme']` without going through `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Skips the blocked-storage fallback and the cross-tab sync in `subscribeTheme`.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme()` from `src/lib/theme.ts`, or `useTheme().toggle()` from `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail soft in the browser. Each capability has a fallback, and nothing throws to the user.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Storage access wrapped in try/catch (`src/lib/theme.ts` `applyTheme`, `storedTheme`). Blocked storage keeps the choice in memory for the page session.\n- `matchMedia` missing or blocked falls back to `light` (`src/lib/theme.ts` `systemTheme`).\n- `IntersectionObserver` missing makes `useActiveSection` a no-op (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n- Motion feature chunk load failure leaves `m` components static, with no unhandled rejection (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` `loadFeatures`).\n- Clipboard failure falls back to `execCommand`, then opens the mail app (`src/lib/copy.ts`).\n- Build-time failures stop the pipeline through the `npm run verify` gates (see `docs/architecture.md` quality gates).\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None at runtime. No `console` calls are part of the design.\n**Validation:** Compile-time only. `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`, plus `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`. Build gates in `scripts/` check the output.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable. Static site.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), `aria-labelledby` on sections, native `<dialog>` focus handling, reduced-motion guard in `src/styles/base.css`, WCAG AA contrast gate (`scripts/check-contrast.mjs`).\n\n## Documentation drift\n\n`docs/architecture.md` (status \"Accepted\") describes items that the code does not match. Code is authoritative for this map:\n- It lists `lenis` for desktop wheel smoothing. setup.\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, one component per file, matching the export name. Example: `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` exports `Hero`.\n- Hooks: `use` prefix, camelCase `.ts`. Example: `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`.\n- Lib and content modules: lowercase or kebab-case `.ts`. Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/content/education.ts`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`.\n- Vendored primitives: kebab-case `.tsx`. Example: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`.\n- Icons: `<Name>Icon.tsx` under `src/components/ui/icons/`.\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` or `*.test.tsx` in `tests/unit/`, `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`. Kebab-case names.\n- Scripts: `check-*.mjs` under `scripts/`.\n- Mixed casing is present. Section and component folders use PascalCase (`src/sections/Hero/`), while `src/layout/`, `src/lib/`, `src/hooks/` and `src/anim/` files use kebab or camel case. `scripts/check-path-case.mjs` enforces that import paths match on-disk case, so keep case exact.\n\n**Directories:**\n- Top-level code folders are lowercase (`src/app`, `src/lib`, `src/anim`). Section folders under `src/sections/` are PascalCase.\n- Test folders are lowercase (`tests/unit`, `tests/e2e`).\n\n**Exports:**\n- Named exports throughout (`export function Hero`). Default exports only where a library requires them (`src/anim/features.ts` exports the feature bundle as `default`).\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New Section:**\n- Implementation: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`. Accept data as props.\n- Data: add a module under `src/content/` and export it from `src/content/index.ts` (`siteContent`). Add a type to `src/content/schema.ts`.\n- Wiring: render it in `src/app/Page.tsx` and add its id to `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`) if it should appear in navigation.\n- Tests: `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`.\n\n**New Component (shared UI):**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`.\n- Icons: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx`, as an inline SVG component.\n- Tests: `tests/unit/` for logic, `tests/unit/layout.test.tsx` pattern for rendering.\n\n**New Animated Behavior:**\n- Wrapper: add to `src/anim/declarative/` (motion) or `src/anim/physics/` (react-spring), and export through `src/anim/index.ts`.\n- Use springs and durations from `src/anim/tokens.ts`. Read the motion preference through `useMotionPreference()`.\n- Do not import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` anywhere else.\n\n**New Content Data:**\n- Module: `src/content/<area>.ts` with `satisfies` against types in `src/content/schema.ts`.\n- Images: register in `src/content/images.ts` with imagetools `?w=...&format=avif;webp&as=picture` imports. Source files go in `src/assets/images/`.\n- Rule: never import content data from anything except `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n\n**New Utility or Theme Helper:**\n- Framework-free: `src/lib/<name>.ts`.\n- Stateful React hook: `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts`.\n- Shared `cn()` already exists in `src/lib/utils.ts`.\n\n**New Design Token:**\n- Role colors and derived values: `src/styles/tokens.css` (light and dark blocks). Map to a Tailwind utility in `src/styles/index.css` (`@theme inline`).\n- Do not add raw hex or palette classes in components (`scripts/check-colors.mjs`).\n\n**New Test:**\n- Unit: `tests/unit/<name>.test.ts(x)`, following the existing kebab-case names.\n- E2E: `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`.
